user: [tool_result] 1	# QDS-Factory Operational Memory Substrate — Implementation Plan & Provenance
2	
3	**Document ID**: QDS-MEM-003-IMPL
4	**Status**: Approved implementation plan (PhD reference); analysis-complete, story-authoring deferred to a later session
5	**Created**: 2026-06-19
6	**Authority**: Implementation record for the operational memory tier — provenance, teardown, divergence map, 13-story breakdown, invariants. Reconciles and sits beneath [QDS-MEM-003](operational-memory-design.md).
7	**Relationship**: Companion to [QDS-MEM-003](operational-memory-design.md) (the design). This doc records *how the substrate is sourced* (**vendored**, not greenfield) and *how the 13-story Phase-2 epic is decomposed and sequenced*. QDS-MEM-003 §11's design stories (P0-OM-1…P4-OM-15, 15 stories / 47 pts) are re-anchored into the 13-story P2.0–P2.12 Phase-2 epic (~44 pts) recorded in §D. **No P0/P1 runtime story changes**; the epic is purely additive and wires in by dependency only.
8	
9	---
10	
11	## 0. TL;DR
12	
13	The operational memory substrate is **vendored** from [ArtemisAI/pi-mem](https://github.com/ArtemisAI/pi-mem) (a claude-mem v10.5.2-lineage retarget for pi-agents), by **copy-paste + adaptation** — **not** a git fork (no remote, no upstream). The pi-mem capture layer is **stripped**; only the swarmclaw/DAG/A-B-specific parts are **greenfield**. This corrects QDS-MEM-003's stale "greenfield, designed from scratch" framing (3×: exec summary L17, architecture diagram L72, decisions table L361) — the reconciliation edit is recorded in §F and applied to QDS-MEM-003 itself.
14	
15	**Scope (user-confirmed): all 13 stories / ~44 pts — no trim.** The 44 pts is not bulk-vendoring. Per-story port-vs-new split (§D):
16	
17	| Class | ~Pts | What | Stories |
18	|---|---|---|---|
19	| **Vendor** | ~10 | substrate lift/adapt (stores, migrations, queue, search framework, MCP, context injection) | P2.0, P2.1, P2.2 |
20	| **Glue** | ~9 | fresh-but-thin capture adapters on the **free** pi-mono ExtensionAPI seam | P2.3, P2.5, P2.6 |
21	| **Net-new** | ~25 | A/B lifecycle + nightly promotion (replaces stripped per-event LLM) + swarmclaw DAG capture + Hindsight bridge | P2.4, P2.7, P2.8, P2.9, P2.10, P2.11, P2.12 |
22	
23	The epic is **additive**: not scheduled into the 4-week P0/P1 runtime window (already at 84-pt stretch). **P2.0 can start once P0.7 exists; the full epic gates on P0.7 + P1.6 + P1.15.**
24	
25	---
26	
27	## A. Provenance
28	
29	### A.1 Substrate source
30	
31	[ArtemisAI/pi-mem](https://github.com/ArtemisAI/pi-mem) — pi-mem is claude-mem v10.5.2 retargeted for pi-agents (669 files, TypeScript, Bun/Node dual runtime, `bun:sqlite`, pipeline: hooks → async queue → LLM worker daemon → SQLite + Chroma → MCP server + hybrid search + context injection).
32	
33	### A.2 Sourcing mode: vendor, not fork
34	
35	pi-mem's substrate is **copy-pasted** into QDS-Factory and **adapted in place**. There is **no git remote, no upstream tracking** — QDS-Factory does not subscribe to pi-mem's future evolution. This is an intentional one-way import: take the battle-tested substrate, then diverge. The fork-vs-vendor distinction matters: "fork" implies a tracked remote we pull from; we do none of that. License attribution is preserved in every vendored file (§A.4).
36	
37	### A.3 Why vendor the substrate, strip the capture
38	
39	- **Vendor the substrate** because rebuilding covers stores / migrations / queue / search / MCP — the genuinely hard parts pi-mem already solved. The greenfield-store alternative (thin extension + new store from scratch) was explicitly rejected (§A.5).
40	- **Strip the capture** because pi-mem's capture is an `SDKAgent` that runs a **per-event Claude call** to distill observations — wrong altitude for a dark factory (Delta 1, load-bearing). pi-mono's `ExtensionAPI` already provides signal acquisition for free (`tool_execution_*`, `turn_*`, `message_update`, `agent_end`), making pi-mem's capture both redundant and cost-wrong.
41	
42	### A.4 License attribution
43	
44	Each vendored file retains its original license header. A `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` entry (created when substrate lands in code) records: ArtemisAI/pi-mem lineage, claude-mem v10.5.2 ancestry, license type, and the import date.
45	
46	### A.5 Build-vs-reuse trade-off (QDS-MEM-003 §10 "Relationship to pi-mem" decision, user-confirmed)
47	
48	| Option | Cost | Risk | Verdict |
49	|---|---|---|---|
50	| **Greenfield store** (thin extension + new store from scratch) | rebuild stores + migrations + queue + search + MCP (the hard parts) | high — re-deriving solved infrastructure | **rejected** |
51	| **Fork pi-mem** (tracked remote, pull upstream) | low | drift coupling; pi-mem's roadmap is not ours | rejected (coupling) |
52	| **Vendor pi-mem substrate** (copy-paste + adapt, strip capture) | lift/adapt substrate + write fresh adapters on free pi-mono seam | low — one-way import, diverge freely | **chosen** (user-confirmed) |
53	
54	---
55	
56	## B. KEEP / STRIP / DEFER Map
57	
58	### B.1 KEEP (vendored substrate — lift + adapt)
59	
60	| Subsystem | pi-mem location | Adaptation |
61	|---|---|---|
62	| SQLite stores + FTS5 | `src/services/sqlite/*` | adapt schema to run/DAG-node/event/experiment model (Delta 2) |
63	| Migrations runner (append-only `.sql`) | migrations dir + runner | adapt to new `schema_meta` |
64	| Async queue processor (backpressure) | `SessionQueueProcessor.ts` | repurpose: queue raw events (no LLM step) |
65	| Search Strategy pattern | interface + SQLite/Chroma/Hybrid backends | **key swap seam**: Chroma backend → sqlite-vec+ONNX; framework kept intact (Delta 3) |
66	| MCP server | `src/servers/mcp-server.ts` | expose recall API |
67	| Context injection formatters | `src/services/context/*` | format run/agent/skill context |
68	| pi-agent ExtensionAPI seam | `pi-agent/extensions/pi-mem.ts` | retarget → capture entry point |
69	
70	### B.2 STRIP (removed — wrong altitude / wrong backend / wrong surface)
71	
72	| Subsystem | Why stripped |
73	|---|---|
74	| `SDKAgent` LLM observation-generator | per-event Claude call — Delta 1 inversion; replaced by mechanical capture + nightly promotion |
75	| Chroma sync + `ChromaMcpManager` | external process → sqlite-vec+ONNX (Delta 3) |
76	| IDE adapters (`src/cli/adapters/*`) | capture surface is swarmclaw+pi-mono, not IDE (Delta 4) |
77	| Transcript watcher/processor (`src/services/transcripts/*`) | same — no IDE transcripts in a DAG factory |
78	
79	### B.3 DEFER (not in Phase-2 epic; plugs in later behind a seam)
80	
81	| Subsystem | Deferral | Seam |
82	|---|---|---|
83	| Worker HTTP/SSE daemon + viewer UI (`src/services/worker/http/*`, `src/ui/viewer/*`) | Phase-4 replay/debug tooling | replay API (P2.12) exposes the data; UI is a later layer |
84	
85	---
86	
87	## C. Seven Deltas — pi-mem → QDS-MEM-003 Architectural Divergence
88	
89	| # | Delta | pi-mem | QDS-MEM-003 | Load-bearing? |
90	|---|---|---|---|---|
91	| **D1** | **Capture altitude inversion** | per-event LLM distillation | **zero** per-event LLM; mechanical raw-event capture; all LLM moves to nightly promotion cron | **YES — drives most fork surgery** |
92	| **D2** | Data model | session/observation/summary/prompt | run / DAG-node / event / experiment / event_edges; `run_id` spine; `parent_event_id` event tree | yes |
93	| **D3** | Vector backend | Chroma (external process) | sqlite-vec + local ONNX `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2` (384-dim); embedded, transactional with event rows, no external API per event | yes (→ SPIKE-11) |
94	| **D4** | Capture surface | IDE transcript-watching | swarmclaw DAG executor + pi-mono `ExtensionAPI` events | yes |
95	| **D5** | DAG-node capture | (no DAG concept in pi-mono) | transitions from swarmclaw orchestration hook surface (shape unknown) | yes (→ SPIKE-10) |
96	| **D6** | Experiment lifecycle | *(nothing analogous)* | full A/B state machine: propose → human gate → execute → deploy → 30-day confirm/auto-rollback | **net-new** |
97	| **D7** | Hindsight promotion | *(nothing analogous)* | nightly cron distills day's events → "Daily Learnings" doc → `retain()` into qds-product/qds-meta; replaces live LLM classification in the current SwarmClaw→Hindsight bridge | **net-new — resolves SPIKE-9** |
98	
99	**D1 is the single most consequential change.** Eliminating per-event LLM and moving all intelligence to a nightly batch fundamentally changes the system's cost profile and data shape. **D7 resolves SPIKE-9**: the SwarmClaw→Hindsight bridge model is **batch (cron)**, not a live LLM handoff.
100	
101	---
102	
103	## D. 13-Story Phase-2 Breakdown (P2.0–P2.12, ~44 pts)
104	
105	Re-anchors QDS-MEM-003 §11's 15 design stories (P0-OM-1…P4-OM-15) into a Phase-2 epic with distinct slugs. Point values are planning estimates. Class = vendor / glue / net-new (§0).
106	
107	### D.0 Correspondence — 15 design stories → 13 Phase-2 stories
108	
109	QDS-MEM-003 §11 decomposes the substrate into **15 design-phase stories** (P0-OM-1…P4-OM-15, 47 pts). This §D re-anchors them into **13 vendoring-aware stories** (P2.0–P2.12, ~44 pts). Points are **re-estimated at the vendor/glue/net-new altitude** (§0) after the pi-mem teardown (§B/§C) — not mechanically redistributed, hence 47 → ~44. The mapping is by capability:
110	
111	| Design story (QDS-MEM-003 §11) | → Phase-2 story | Change | Reason |
112	|---|---|---|---|
113	| P0-OM-1 (store bootstrap) | → P2.0 + P2.1 + P2.2 | **split 1→3** | vendoring splits the monolithic bootstrap into toolchain lift (P2.0), store + vector (P2.1), migrations runner (P2.2) |
114	| P0-OM-2 (tool-call capture) | → P2.3 | 1:1 | glue adapter on the pi-mono ExtensionAPI seam |
115	| P0-OM-3 (recall API) | → P2.1 (absorbed) | **merged** | recall + vector search live inside the event-store story; no standalone recall slice |
116	| P1-OM-4 (DAG-node capture) | → P2.4 | 1:1 (3→4) | net-new; gated on SPIKE-10 |
117	| P1-OM-5 (reasoning capture) | → P2.5 | 1:1 | glue |
118	| P1-OM-6 (outcome capture) | → P2.6 | 1:1 | glue |
119	| P2-OM-7 (nightly cron) | → P2.7 | 1:1 (3→4) | net-new; replaces stripped per-event LLM (D1) |
120	| P2-OM-8 (Hindsight bridge) | → P2.8 | 1:1 | net-new |
121	| P3-OM-9 (experiment propose) + P3-OM-10 (human gate) | → P2.9 | **merge 2→1** | propose + gate are one workflow unit |
122	| P3-OM-11 (A/B execution) + P3-OM-12 (winner deploy) | → P2.10 | **merge 2→1** | execute + deploy are one variant-tagged-DAG story (5 pts) |
123	| P3-OM-13 (30-day confirm) | → P2.11 | 1:1 (4→3) | net-new |
124	| P4-OM-14 (replay/debug) + P4-OM-15 (retention/observability) | → P2.12 | **merge 2→1** | both are housekeeping over the same event store |
125	
126	**Net: 15 → 13** (+2 from the P0-OM-1 split; −4 from four merges: P0-OM-3 absorbed into P2.1, P3-OM-9+10→P2.9, P3-OM-11+12→P2.10, P4-OM-14+15→P2.12).
127	
128	### D.1 Phase 2a — Foundation & Intake (5 stories / 17 pts)
129	
130	| Story | Title | Pts | Class | Notes |
131	|---|---|---|---|---|
132	| **P2.0** | Toolchain bootstrap | 3 | vendor | substrate copy-paste; Bun/Node dual-runtime wiring; deps (sqlite-vec, ONNX runtime); license attribution (§A.4) |
133	| **P2.1** | Event store (SQLite + sqlite-vec) | 4 | vendor | lift SQLite+FTS5 stores; adapt schema to run/DAG-node/event model (D2); `event_vec` virtual table (D3) |
134	| **P2.2** | Schema migration runner | 3 | vendor | lift append-only `.sql` runner; adapt `schema_meta` |
135	| **P2.3** | Tool-call capture (pi-mono ExtensionAPI) | 3 | glue | zero-LLM threading; ONNX embed; one tool call → one `events` + one `event_vec` row, transactional. **DoD: zero LLM/HTTP egress (network mock asserts); p99 < 5 ms over 1000 calls; ONNX deterministic 384-dim** |
136	| **P2.4** | DAG-node capture (swarmclaw seam) | 4 | net-new | **gated on SPIKE-10** (swarmclaw DAG hook surface); node transitions + handoffs → `node` events; event tree via `parent_event_id` (D5) |
137	
138	### D.2 Phase 2b — Recall & Promote (4 stories / 13 pts)
139	
140	| Story | Title | Pts | Class | Notes |
141	|---|---|---|---|---|
142	| **P2.5** | Reasoning + agent-context capture | 3 | glue | pi-mono message events → `message` events; vector-indexed |
143	| **P2.6** | Outcome capture | 3 | glue | test/review/build adapters → `outcome` events; linked to run |
144	| **P2.7** | Nightly promotion cron | 4 | net-new | **replaces stripped per-event LLM (D1)**; light consolidation of day's events → "Daily Learnings" doc |
145	| **P2.8** | Hindsight retain bridge | 3 | net-new | push daily doc → `qds-product`/`qds-meta` `retain` w/ correct tags. **Cross-epic dep: P1.11** (Hindsight retain, KEEP). Resolves SPIKE-9 (D7) |
146	
147	### D.3 Phase 2c — Experiment (3 stories / 11 pts)
148	
149	| Story | Title | Pts | Class | Notes |
150	|---|---|---|---|---|
151	| **P2.9** | Experiment propose + human gate | 3 | net-new | optimizer agent scans runs, proposes hypothesis → `experiments` (proposed); CLI/UI approve/reject + audit event → approved |
152	| **P2.10** | A/B execution + deploy | 5 | net-new | **E2E gated on swarmclaw variant-tagged DAGs (SPIKE-10)**; ships interface + unit tests first; full E2E when seam confirmed; measures metric, declares winner → analyzed; deploy winner + snapshot baseline + `confirm_until=+30d` → deployed |
153	| **P2.11** | Confirm cron (30-day auto-rollback) | 3 | net-new | post-deploy vs baseline; confirm/rollback + Hindsight qds-meta finding → confirmed/rolled_back |
154	
155	### D.4 Phase 2d — Housekeeping (1 story / 3 pts)
156	
157	| Story | Title | Pts | Class | Notes |
158	|---|---|---|---|---|
159	| **P2.12** | Replay + retention policy | 3 | net-new | reconstruct any run's event timeline/tree (serves optimizer + humans); operational-store cleanup/decay policy |
160	
161	**Totals: 13 stories · ~44 pts · vendor ~10 · glue ~9 · net-new ~25.**
162	
163	### D.5 Sequencing DAG
164	
165	```
166	P0.7 (SwarmClaw agent+MCP registry) ── epic entry gate ──▶ P2.0 (toolchain)
167	                                                             │
168	                          ┌──────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────┐
169	                          ▼                                                                     ▼
170	                P2.1 (event store + vec)                                                P2.2 (migrations runner)
171	                ◀── SPIKE-11 (sqlite-vec/ONNX, D3)
172	                    │
173	        ┌───────────┼───────────┐   ◀── fan out once P2.1 exists
174	        ▼           ▼           ▼
175	      P2.3        P2.5        P2.6            (glue capture adapters: tool / reasoning / outcome)
176	
177	SPIKE-10 ──▶ P2.4 (net-new, DAG-node capture)                 ◀── D5 depends (P2.4 also needs P2.1)
178	
179	P2.3/P2.5/P2.6 (capture live) ──▶ P2.7 (nightly cron) ──▶ P2.8 (Hindsight bridge)
180	P1.11 (KEEP, Hindsight retain) ──▶ P2.8          ◀── cross-epic dependency
181	
182	P2.8 ──▶ P2.9 ──▶ P2.10 (gated SPIKE-10) ──▶ P2.11
183	P2.1 ──▶ P2.12 (replay/retention)
184	
185	Full epic gates on: P0.7 + P1.6 (Warren dispatch) + P1.15 (real PR → real runs to capture/promote/experiment on)
186	```
187	
188	**Parallelism:** P2.3/P2.5/P2.6 (capture adapters) fan out once P2.1 exists; P2.7/P2.8 run once capture + P1.11 are live.
189	
190	---
191	
192	## E. Invariants & Verification
193	
194	### E.1 Operational-memory invariants (carry into every Phase-2 story's DoD)
195	
196	| ID | Invariant |
197	|---|---|
198	| **INV-OM1** | **Zero per-event LLM/HTTP** — capture is mechanical; no network egress during capture (network mock asserts). The only LLM work is nightly promotion (P2.7) + per-experiment optimization (P2.9–P2.11). |
199	| **INV-OM2** | **`run_id` non-null** on every `events` row — the spine; capture adapters omitting it are defective by definition. |
200	| **INV-OM3** | **`payload_json`/`result_json` ≤ 64 KB, secret-pattern-masked** — bounded rows; secrets scrubbed before write. |
201	| **INV-OM4** | **ONNX model from a pinned local path only — no remote fetch** — deterministic local inference, no external API per event (D3). |
202	
203	### E.2 Verification (docs-only, this session)
204	
205	- `grep -rn "greenfield" docs/planning/operational-memory-design.md` → only the RuFlo-contrast sense remains; no "designed from scratch" substrate claim.
206	- `grep -n "ArtemisAI/pi-mem" docs/planning/operational-memory-design.md` → present in §14; same in this doc §A.

# QDS-Factory P-Numbered Story Template (extracted from real repo convention)

## COPY-PASTEABLE SKELETON

```markdown
# P2.{N}: {Title — imperative, capability-named}

**Story Points:** {2|3|4}

## Spec Reference

- PRD §{X} ({Section name}); {FR-XXX-N}, {NFR-XXX-N}
- Phase exit gate: PRD §13 Phase 2 clause {N}

## User Story

As a {Role},
I want {capability},
so that {business outcome} ({GoalTag — short rationale}).

## Overview

{3–6 sentences. What this story wires end-to-end, which layers it touches, why it is a {N}-point atomic unit. State that it closes wiring with no stub/TODO.}

## E2E Slice

**Demonstrable behavior:** {Concrete, runnable sequence with real endpoints/tools and an asserted observable result.}

**Wiring closed:** On completion of this story, {behavior} using only code merged by this story{ and predecessor P2.X}. No stub, no `TODO: wire later`, and no sibling story is required to make this behavior real.

**Dependency edges (predecessors):** {P2.X, P2.Y | None — standalone.}

## Acceptance Criteria

> ≥3 scenarios, Given/When/Then. Exactly one tagged (make-or-break) and binary.

1. **(make-or-break)** Given {precondition}, When {action}, Then {binary, asserted outcome}.
2. Given {…}, When {…}, Then {…}.
3. Given {…}, When {…}, Then {…}.
{4–7 typical; failure-path and guard scenarios encouraged.}

## Technical Notes

- **Contracts/Schema:** {Zod schemas in a ```typescript block``` for infra stories, OR an inline `{ field: type, ... }` shape for glue stories.}
- **Integration points{ (each wired & exercised in this story)}:** {endpoints, MCP tools, subprocesses, banks — bulleted or inline.}
- **Files / functions touched:** {`path.ts` (purpose), `path2.ts` (purpose), ...}
- **Guards/limits:** {timeouts, caps, size limits, idempotency, atomicity rules.}

## Files to Modify

> ≤10 unique paths, one per line.
- `src/{...}.ts`
- `tests/{...}.test.ts`

## Security Invariants

> Concrete named invariants only — N/A NOT permitted when triggered.

- **INV-{TAG}1:** {Concrete enforced invariant.}
- **INV-{TAG}2:** {…}

## Definition of Done

> {8–12 | 18–22} high-signal, story-specific boxes. NO lint/tests/typecheck filler.

- [ ] **Impl:** {…}
- [ ] **Impl:** {…}
- [ ] **Contract:** {schemas validated on all inputs/routes}
- [ ] **Integration:** {layer wired & exercised end-to-end}
- [ ] **Security:** {INV-{TAG}1 through INV-{TAG}N enforced — concrete enumeration}
- [ ] **Test (unit):** {…}
- [ ] **Test (e2e demo — MANDATORY):** {full runnable round-trip with assertion}

## Dev Agent Record

<!-- Empty placeholder — filled during development. -->
```

---

## CONVENTION NOTES (with file:line citations)

**1. Section structure & order (verbatim, in this exact sequence):**
`# P{n}.{m}: Title` → `**Story Points:**` → `## Spec Reference` → `## User Story` → `## Overview` → `## E2E Slice` → `## Acceptance Criteria` → `## Technical Notes` → `## Files to Modify` → `## Security Invariants` → `## Definition of Done` → `## Dev Agent Record`. Confirmed identical across all 7 files read (p0-9:1-330, p1-13:1-71, p0-4:1-73).
- ALWAYS present: title, Story Points, Spec Reference, User Story, Overview, E2E Slice, Acceptance Criteria, Technical Notes, Files to Modify, Security Invariants, Definition of Done.
- CONDITIONAL / story-specific extra `##` sections appear ONLY between Technical Notes and Files to Modify (or inside Technical Notes as `###`): e.g. p0-7 has `## Cross-Cutting Convention Notes` (p0-7:195) and `## Observability Contract (CC-9)` (p0-7:199); p1-4 has `## Phase-to-PI Extension Mapping` (p1-4:41), `## Phase Algorithms` (p1-4:70), `## Phase Config Binding` (p1-4:103), `## CC-9 Observability Contract` (p1-4:150). Do not add these unless the story needs them.
- `## Dev Agent Record` with the HTML comment placeholder is the trailing section on the smaller/glue stories (p1-14:69-71, p0-4:70-72, p1-4:176-178). The large infra stories p0-7 and p0-9 OMIT it (end at DoD — p0-7:248, p0-9:330). Both are valid; include it for ≤12-DoD stories.

**2. Header / front-matter format:** NO YAML front-matter. Line 1 is `# P{phase}.{num}: {Title}` (p0-7:1, p1-14:1, p0-9:1). Line 3 is `**Story Points:** {int}` (p0-7:3, p1-14:3). Story ID uses dot notation `P0.7`, `P1.14` in the heading. Spec Reference is a 2–3 line bullet list: PRD section + FR/NFR codes (p1-14:7), and `Phase exit gate: PRD §13 Phase {N} clause {n}` (p1-14:8). Note p1-13:5-7 OMITS the phase-exit-gate line (a deferred-gate story) — so that line is conditional.

**3. User-story phrasing:** Three lines, comma-terminated, lowercase "so that":
`As a {Role},` / `I want {…},` / `so that {…}{ ({GoalTag} — {rationale})}.` (p0-7:12-14, p0-9:12-14, p1-13:11-13). Role is bolded ONLY in the prose-heavy variant via `**Role**`? — No: in these files the Role is NOT bolded (plain `As a QDS dev agent,` p0-9:12). Optional trailing goal tag in parens, e.g. `(G2 — shared substrate)` (p0-7:14), `(G1 — reliable memory)` (p0-9:14).

**4. Acceptance Criteria format:** Numbered list (`1.`, `2.`…), Given/When/Then prose (not Gherkin blocks). Exactly ONE item tagged `**(make-or-break)**` and it must be binary — always item 1 (p0-7:32, p0-9:32, p1-14:30, p1-13:29, p0-4:33). Optional blockquote guide line precedes the list on infra stories: `> ≥3 scenarios, Given/When/Then. Exactly one tagged (make-or-break) and binary.` (p0-7:30, p0-9:30). Typical count: 3–7 (glue stories 4–5: p1-14 has 4, p1-13 has 5, p0-4 has 3; infra stories 6–7: p0-7 has 7, p1-4 has 7).

**5. Technical Notes structure:** Bulleted with bold lead-ins. Canonical four bullets: `**Contracts/Schema:**`, `**Integration points:**` (infra variant `**Integration points (each wired & exercised in this story):**`), `**Files / functions touched:**`, `**Guards/limits:**` (p1-14:37-40, p1-13:37-40, p0-4:39-42). Infra stories embed fenced ```typescript Zod schema blocks (p0-7:43-130, p0-9:45-131); glue stories use inline `{ field: type }` shapes (p1-14:37, p1-13:37). `### `-level sub-sections (e.g. `### Mulch Storage Format Contract` p0-9:133, `### Bank ID Resolution (CC-6)` p0-9:213) appear inside Technical Notes only on complex stories.

**6. Files to Modify:** `## Files to Modify`, then optional guide blockquote `> ≤10 unique paths, one per line.` (p0-7:165, p0-9:295), then a bulleted list of backtick-quoted paths, one per line (p0-7:166-174, p1-14:44-47, p1-13:44-51). Includes BOTH `src/` and `tests/` paths. Counts observed: p1-14=4, p1-11=5, p0-4=6, p1-13=8, p0-7=9, p0-9=9 — all ≤10. p1-4 omits the blockquote and adds a trailing note that the list is "illustrative" (p1-4:142) — discouraged; prefer concrete ≤10.

**7. Security Invariants:** `## Security Invariants`, optional guide blockquote `> Concrete named invariants only — N/A NOT permitted when triggered.` (p0-7:178, p0-9:308), then bulleted named invariants. Two naming styles in use: bolded domain-tagged `**INV-SC1:**`/`**INV-HM1:**` on infra stories (p0-7:180, p0-9:310) and plain `INV-1:`/`INV-COST-1:` on glue stories (p1-14:51, p1-13:55, p1-11:51). For a memory/backend P2 story, prefer a domain tag (e.g. `INV-OM1`).

**8. Definition of Done:** `## Definition of Done` + guide blockquote stating box-count + "NO lint/tests/typecheck filler", then `- [ ]` checkboxes each prefixed with a bold **category:**. Two size bands, both real:
- Glue/router stories use **8–12 boxes** — p1-14:55-67 (11 boxes), p1-13:60-70 (9 boxes), p1-11:55-65 (9), p0-4:60-68 (8). p0-9 explicitly sets `> 8–12 high-signal...` (p0-9:317) and lands at 11 (p0-9:319-330).
- Heavy infra stories use **18–22 boxes** — p0-7:224 guide `> 18–22 high-signal...` with 22 boxes (p0-7:226-248).
- Category labels actually used (verbatim): `**Impl:**`, `**Contract:**`, `**Integration:**`, `**Security:**`, `**Observability:**` (p0-4:65), `**Test (unit):**`, `**Test (unit/integration):**`, `**Test (validation guard):**` / `**Test (guard):**`, `**Test (allowlist coverage):**`, `**Test (idempotency):**`, `**Test (lifecycle):**`, `**Test (e2e demo — MANDATORY):**`.
- The **`Test (e2e demo — MANDATORY)`** box is ALWAYS the last/penultimate DoD item and is present in every story (p0-7:239, p0-9:327, p1-14:67, p1-13:70, p1-11:65, p0-4:68, p1-4:171).
- **High-signal count for the atomicity audit (≤10 target):** These DoD lists already contain ZERO project-wide CI filler (no "ruff clean / tests pass / mypy clean" lines — the guide blockquote explicitly forbids them). So for the glue band, the high-signal count == the literal box count: p1-14=11, p1-13=9, p1-11=9, p0-4=8 high-signal items. To hit ≤10 cleanly, target the 8–12 band and keep it at the low end (8–10 boxes). The 18–22 infra band intentionally exceeds 10 because each box is a distinct security/guard test for a net-new-infra story.

**9. Story-points & class/sub-phase labeling:** Points are `**Story Points:** {2|3|4}` only — observed values 3 and 4 across phase-0/1 (p0-4:3=3, p1-14:3=3, p0-7:3=4, p0-9:3=4, p1-13:3=4). No 1-point or 5+-point stories in the read set. There is NO front-matter "Class" or "Sub-phase" field — the vendor/glue/net-new distinction is conveyed implicitly via the Overview prose (e.g. "4-point atomic unit because it touches… three layers" p0-7:18) and via DoD size band, NOT a structured label. Predecessor/dependency class is carried by `**Dependency edges (predecessors):**` in the E2E Slice (p0-7:26, p1-14:26, p0-9:26).

**10. Slug / filename convention:** CONFIRMED `p{phase}-{n}-{title-kebab}.md`, lowercase, hyphen between phase and number, hyphen-kebab title. Examples on disk: `p0-7-swarmclaw-agent-mcp-hindsight.md`, `p1-14-outcome-record-router.md`, `p0-9-hindsight-banks-mulch.md`, `p1-4-protocol-template.md`. So P2 stories must be `p2-<n>-<title-kebab>.md` in `_bmad-output/sprint-artifacts/phase-2/stories/`. NOTE the in-file heading uses a DOT (`# P2.7:`) while the filename uses a HYPHEN (`p2-7-...md`) — this dot-vs-hyphen split is consistent across every file.

---

### Additional load-bearing conventions for P2 writers
- **E2E Slice three-part block is mandatory and verbatim-labeled:** `**Demonstrable behavior:**`, `**Wiring closed:**`, `**Dependency edges (predecessors):**` (p0-7:22/24/26, p0-9:22/24/26, p1-14:22/24/26). The "Wiring closed" sentence template ("…using only code merged by this story… No stub, no `TODO: wire later`, and no sibling story is required to make this behavior real.") is near-verbatim reused — copy it.
- **Predecessor reconciliation note:** When a predecessor is in the DEFER set, add a `> **Predecessor reconciliation (PR #21, QDS-EXEC-001):**` blockquote (p1-4:29, p0-4:29) rather than silently pointing at a deferred story. Relevant because QDS-MEM-003's entry-gate dependency is P0.7 (net-new infra) — verify P0.7 is in the KEEP set before citing it as a predecessor.
- **Cross-story schema references** are cited by schema name + owning story (e.g. "validated against P0.9's `BankIdSchema`" p0-7:146). P2 stories depending on P0.7/P0.9 memory contracts should reference `BankIdSchema`, `RetainPayloadSchema`, `MCPInvokeSchema` by name and owner, not redefine them.

| **P2.8** | Hindsight retain bridge | 3 | net-new | 2b | P2.7; **P1.11** [cross-epic, Hindsight retain KEEP] |
| **P2.9** | Experiment propose + human gate | 3 | net-new | 2c | P2.8 |
| **P2.10** | A/B execution + deploy | 5 | net-new | 2c | P2.9 **+ SPIKE-10** [spike, variant-tagged DAGs — E2E BLOCKING] |
| **P2.11** | Confirm cron (30-day auto-rollback) | 3 | net-new | 2c | P2.10 |
| **P2.12** | Replay + retention policy | 3 | net-new | 2d | P2.1 |

**Cross-epic / spike gates (full epic):** P0.7 + P1.6 (Warren dispatch) + P1.15 (real PR → real runs). Spike-gated stories: **P2.4** (SPIKE-10) and **P2.10** (SPIKE-10). Spike-informed: **P2.1** (SPIKE-11, not blocking — substrate exists in pi-mem).

**Build-order topological layers (for reference, not writing order):**
`P0.7 → P2.0 → {P2.1, P2.2}` · `P2.1 → {P2.3, P2.4(+SPIKE-10), P2.5, P2.6, P2.12}` · `{P2.3,P2.5,P2.6} → P2.7 → P2.8(+P1.11) → P2.9 → P2.10(+SPIKE-10) → P2.11`

## Part 2 — Seven Writing-Agent Batches (≤2 stories each, grouped by affinity)

Grouping principle: each agent's 2 stories share research context (same subsystem / class / sub-phase) so the agent loads one mental model and writes both specs against it. 13 stories ÷ 7 agents = six pairs + one solo (6×2 + 1×1 = 13). ✓

| Batch | Agent | Stories | Pts | Class affinity | One-line rationale |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **W-A** | Vendor-substrate | **P2.0 + P2.1** | 7 | vendor | Toolchain bootstrap and the event store it enables are one substrate-lift unit (copy-paste deps → first store + `event_vec`); same pi-mem `src/services/sqlite/*` research context. P2.1 carries the SPIKE-11 note. |
| **W-B** | Vendor-migrations | **P2.2 + P2.12** | 6 | vendor + housekeeping | Both are append-only-`.sql` / `schema_meta` schema-mechanics stories over the same store; P2.12 retention/decay is schema-lifecycle work that pairs naturally with the migration runner. Both depend only on P2.0/P2.1 (no deep chain). |
| **W-C** | Glue-capture-1 | **P2.3 + P2.5** | 6 | glue | Two pi-mono `ExtensionAPI` capture adapters (tool-call + reasoning/message events); identical zero-LLM threading + ONNX-embed pattern, shared INV-OM1/OM2/OM4 DoD. |
| **W-D** | Glue-capture-2 + spike | **P2.6 + P2.4** | 7 | glue + net-new (spike) | P2.6 outcome capture is the third glue adapter (same seam); P2.4 DAG-node capture is the capture-surface sibling on the swarmclaw seam. Co-locating all "capture" stories keeps the event-shape model in one agent. **P2.4 carries the SPIKE-10 blocked-by note.** |
| **W-E** | Promote-bridge | **P2.7 + P2.8** | 7 | net-new (promotion) | Nightly cron → Hindsight retain is one promotion pipeline (D7/SPIKE-9 resolution); P2.8 needs P2.7's daily-doc output shape + the P1.11 cross-epic dep. Single research context: cron + retain bridge. |
| **W-F** | Experiment-core + spike | **P2.9 + P2.10** | 8 | net-new (A/B) | Propose+gate and execute+deploy are the contiguous front of the A/B state machine (proposed → approved → analyzed → deployed); same `experiments` table + lifecycle research. **P2.10 carries the SPIKE-10 blocked-by note + interface-first DoD.** |
| **W-G** | Experiment-confirm (solo) | **P2.11** | 3 | net-new (A/B) | 30-day confirm/auto-rollback is the tail of the A/B machine (deployed → confirmed/rolled_back); the natural solo because 13 is odd and confirm is a self-contained cron that shares the A/B model already in W-F's context. |

**Affinity check vs. prompt suggestions:** vendor-pair satisfied (W-A = P2.0+P2.1); glue-capture-pair satisfied (W-C = P2.3+P2.5; P2.6 grouped with capture-sibling P2.4 in W-D); experiment-pair satisfied (W-F = P2.9+P2.10, P2.11 solo in W-G). The only deviation from the literal hint is pairing P2.12 with P2.2 (both schema-mechanics) rather than with P2.11 — chosen because P2.12 is housekeeping over the *store/schema* (W-B's domain), not over the A/B lifecycle, giving tighter research-context affinity and isolating the two spike-gated stories into different batches (P2.4→W-D, P2.10→W-F) so no single agent owns both spike risks.

---

## Part 3 — Write-Wave Ordering

**Key insight (verified):** writing produces *story spec Markdown*, not executing code. No batch consumes another batch's runtime artifact, so the build-order DAG does **not** constrain writing order. The only inter-batch coupling is *narrative consistency* (a later spec should reference the interface a dependency spec defines), which is handled by giving every agent the §D doc + this dependency table as shared input — not by serializing.

**Recommendation: dispatch all 7 batches in a single parallel wave (Wave 1).**

```
WAVE 1 (parallel — all 7 agents dispatched in ONE message):
  W-A (P2.0+P2.1)   W-B (P2.2+P2.12)   W-C (P2.3+P2.5)
  W-D (P2.6+P2.4)   W-E (P2.7+P2.8)    W-F (P2.9+P2.10)
  W-G (P2.11)
```

Per CLAUDE.md "1 MESSAGE = ALL RELATED OPERATIONS": spawn all 7 writing agents concurrently via the Task tool in one message.

**Shared-context requirement (replaces serialization):** every agent receives, as input, (a) §D of the implementation plan, (b) the Part-1 dependency table above, and (c) the relevant INV-OM1–OM4 invariants for its stories' DoD. This lets a downstream-spec agent (e.g., W-E writing P2.8) reference the upstream interface (P2.7's daily-doc shape) by reading the *plan*, not by waiting for W-E's own P2.7 prose. The dependency edges become "cite the interface" requirements in the spec, satisfied at the documentation level.

**If a staged fallback is ever needed** (e.g., to let interface decisions in foundation specs settle before dependent specs cite them), the minimal 3-stage ordering by dependency depth would be:
- Stage 1 (foundation interfaces): W-A, W-B, W-C, W-D
- Stage 2 (promotion, cites capture): W-E
- Stage 3 (experiment, cites bridge): W-F, W-G

But staging is **not recommended** — parallel is correct for a docs-writing wave and is faster.

## Part 5 — Spike-Gated Stories (BLOCKING flags for writing agents)

Two stories are SPIKE-10-gated. Their writing agents (W-D for P2.4, W-F for P2.10) **must** add an explicit blocked-by note and ship an interface-first Definition of Done so the spec is build-ready the moment SPIKE-10 lands.

| Story | Batch | Spike | Required spec language |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P2.4** DAG-node capture | **W-D** | SPIKE-10 (D5 — swarmclaw DAG hook surface, shape unknown) | Add header note: **"BLOCKED-BY SPIKE-10 (swarmclaw DAG event-hook surface)."** DoD = interface-first: define the `node` event shape + `parent_event_id` event-tree contract + adapter signature against the *assumed* hook surface; full capture wiring deferred until SPIKE-10 confirms the seam. Also still needs P2.1 (event store). |
| **P2.10** A/B execution + deploy | **W-F** | SPIKE-10 (variant-tagged DAGs) | Add header note: **"BLOCKED-BY SPIKE-10 (variant-tagged DAG execution)."** DoD = interface + unit tests first (state transitions proposed→…→deployed, winner declaration, baseline snapshot, `confirm_until=+30d`); full E2E deferred until the variant-tagged-DAG seam is confirmed. This matches §D.3's existing "ships interface + unit tests first; full E2E when seam confirmed" wording — the agent should carry it verbatim into the DoD. |

Note for W-A: P2.1 is **SPIKE-11**-informed (sqlite-vec/ONNX in Bun) but *not* blocking — the substrate already exists in pi-mem, so SPIKE-11 is a validation, not a gate. W-A should add a lighter note: **"SPIKE-11 validates sqlite-vec+ONNX MiniLM in Bun; substrate vendored, so de-risk not block."** Enforce INV-OM4 (pinned local ONNX path, no remote fetch) in P2.1's DoD.

# QDS-MEM-003 Phase-2 Epic — Per-Story Design Brief (P2.0–P2.12)

**For:** Story-writing agents (Track A backend). Every block below is grounded in a doc§section citation so you can populate real `**Spec Reference:**` lines.

**Doc IDs / files:**
- **QDS-MEM-003** = `/Users/steve/QDS-Factory/docs/planning/operational-memory-design.md` (the design)
- **QDS-MEM-003-IMPL** = `/Users/steve/QDS-Factory/docs/planning/operational-memory-implementation-plan.md` (provenance, KEEP/STRIP/DEFER §B, Seven Deltas §C, 13-story §D, invariants §E)
- **QDS-MEM-001** = `/Users/steve/QDS-Factory/docs/planning/hindsight-memory-master-design.md`
- **QDS-EXEC-001** = `/Users/steve/QDS-Factory/docs/planning/qds-factory-4-week-execution-and-spike-plan.md`

## GLOBAL BLOCK — applies to EVERY story

### G.1 The Four Invariants (INV-OM1..INV-OM4) — verbatim, into EVERY story's DoD
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §E.1

| ID | Invariant (verbatim) |
|---|---|
| **INV-OM1** | **Zero per-event LLM/HTTP** — capture is mechanical; no network egress during capture (network mock asserts). The only LLM work is nightly promotion (P2.7) + per-experiment optimization (P2.9–P2.11). |
| **INV-OM2** | **`run_id` non-null** on every `events` row — the spine; capture adapters omitting it are defective by definition. |
| **INV-OM3** | **`payload_json`/`result_json` ≤ 64 KB, secret-pattern-masked** — bounded rows; secrets scrubbed before write. |
| **INV-OM4** | **ONNX model from a pinned local path only — no remote fetch** — deterministic local inference, no external API per event (D3). |

Applicability note for writers: INV-OM2/INV-OM3 bind hardest on capture stories (P2.3–P2.6); INV-OM4 binds on store/embed stories (P2.1, P2.3, P2.5); INV-OM1 binds on every capture/recall story and is the *negative* DoD for the whole tier. The threading contract that backs INV-OM2 is spelled out at QDS-MEM-003 §5.2 ("Capture adapters that omit these fields are defective by definition").

### G.2 A/B Experiment State Machine (for P2.9–P2.11)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §8.1 (diagram) + §8.2 (autonomy decision) + §4.1 `experiments` table CHECK constraint + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §C D6

Canonical status enum (from the `experiments.status` CHECK, QDS-MEM-003 §4.1 L177-179):
`proposed → approved → running → analyzed → deployed → confirming → confirmed | rolled_back`; plus `rejected` (exits at the gate). `winner ∈ {a, b, none}`.

Transition ownership:
- **propose** → `proposed` (optimizer agent scans recent runs, writes `experiments` row) — P2.9
- **HUMAN GATE** (the ONE human gate, §8.2) → `approved | rejected` — P2.9
- **execute** → `running → analyzed` (swarmclaw DAG, N variant-tagged runs each, measures metric, declares winner) — P2.10
- **deploy** → `deployed` (apply winning config, `deploy_ref` set, `baseline_metrics_json` snapshotted, `confirm_until = deployed_at + 30d`) — P2.10
- **confirm** → `confirming → confirmed | rolled_back` (30-day cron, post-deploy vs baseline; auto-rollback on regression) — P2.11

Key design constraints writers must encode: **exactly one human gate at step 2** (a second gate at deploy was *considered and rejected*, §8.2); **automatic rollback** is the longitudinal safety net; **every state transition is itself an event in the operational store** (full audit trail, §8.2); all `confirmed`/`rolled_back` outcomes `retain()` into Hindsight `qds-meta` and feed the Meta-Optimizer's `intervention_effectiveness` analysis (QDS-MEM-003 §8.3, cross-ref QDS-MEM-001 §4.3).

### G.3 Nightly-Promotion-Cron Model (for P2.7/P2.8)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §7.1 + §7.2 + Decisions table §10 ("Promotion model" / "Promotion depth") + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §C D1/D7

The cron (one run/night, off-peak): (1) reads previous day's events (bounded window — scales with **days, not events**); (2) **light mechanical consolidation** — dedupe, cluster by run/skill/topic, summarize each cluster (depth = **light** by default; deep synthesis stays in Hindsight's weekly Synthesizer); (3) emits one **"Daily Learnings YYYY-MM-DD" document** (structured JSON + prose); (4) calls Hindsight `retain()` routing to `qds-product` (decisions/antipatterns/patterns, tags `module:X`/`knowledge_type:Y`) and `qds-meta` (skill/outcome/optimization, tags `skill:X`/`outcome:Y`/`quality_dim:Z`). Idle nights below an event-volume threshold are **skipped**; doc size is **capped**. This is **D1** (all LLM intelligence moved off per-event capture into this batch) and **D7** (resolves SPIKE-9 — the SwarmClaw→Hindsight bridge is **batch cron, not a live hook**). **Cron-term disambiguation:** this nightly promotion cron ≠ the Hindsight strategic cron agents (Synthesizer/Meta-Optimizer/Blind-Spot, weekly/biweekly/monthly) — QDS-MEM-003 §7.1 note.

### G.4 Epic-Level Gating & Cross-Epic Dependencies
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §0 + §D.5 (Sequencing DAG)
- **Epic entry gate:** P2.0 can start once **P0.7** (SwarmClaw agent+MCP registry) exists.
- **Full epic gates on:** **P0.7 + P1.6** (Pi-team dispatch via Warren) **+ P1.15** (real PR → real runs to capture/promote/experiment on).
- **Spikes (Phase-2 gates, outside the 4-week window):** SPIKE-10 (swarmclaw DAG hook surface, QDS-EXEC-001 §7 L585-604), SPIKE-11 (sqlite-vec + ONNX MiniLM under Bun, QDS-EXEC-001 §7 L606-625). SPIKE-9 is **RESOLVED** (QDS-EXEC-001 §7 L552-583).

## PER-STORY BRIEFS

---

### P2.0 — Toolchain bootstrap (3 pts · **vendor**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.1 + §A (provenance) + §A.4 (license)

- **Design intent / scope:** Copy-paste the pi-mem substrate into QDS-Factory; wire Bun/Node dual-runtime; add deps (sqlite-vec, ONNX runtime); record license attribution. **Vendor, not fork** — no git remote, no upstream tracking (§A.2). This is the foundational lift before any store/adapter work.
- **In scope:** substrate copy-paste; dual-runtime wiring; dep install; `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` entry (ArtemisAI/pi-mem lineage, claude-mem v10.5.2 ancestry, license type, import date — §A.4); per-file original license headers retained.
- **Explicitly out:** any schema adaptation (→P2.1), any capture (→P2.3+), the stripped subsystems (see §B.2: `SDKAgent` LLM observer, Chroma/`ChromaMcpManager`, IDE adapters, transcript watcher — these must NOT be vendored).
- **Deltas touched:** none directly; sets the stage for all. Establishes the vendor decision (§A.5 build-vs-reuse: greenfield rejected, fork rejected, vendor chosen — user-confirmed).
- **pi-mem subsystem (KEEP/STRIP):** KEEP all of §B.1 substrate as raw files; STRIP per §B.2 at import time.
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** license header preserved in every vendored file; `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` created when substrate lands.
- **Spike gating:** none.
- **Cross-epic deps:** **needs P0.7** (epic entry gate, §0 / §D.5).

---

### P2.1 — Event store (SQLite + sqlite-vec) (4 pts · **vendor**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §4 (full schema) + §4.1 (core tables) + §4.2 (`event_vec`) + §6 (Recall API — absorbed) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.1 + §C D2/D3 + §D.0 (P0-OM-3 merged in)

- **Design intent / scope:** Lift pi-mem's SQLite+FTS5 stores; **adapt the schema to the run/DAG-node/event/experiment model (D2)**; add the `event_vec` virtual table (D3). **Recall API is absorbed into this story** (§D.0: "recall + vector search live inside the event-store story; no standalone recall slice").
- **In scope:** `events`, `runs`, `experiments`, `event_edges` tables (QDS-MEM-003 §4.1, verbatim DDL incl. all CHECK constraints + indexes); `event_vec USING vec0(... FLOAT[384])` (§4.2); WAL mode; the Recall API (§6): by-run timeline + `parent_event_id` tree reconstruction, by agent/skill/prompt-version/variant/time-window indexed scans, semantic vector search, aggregations (outcome rates, token/duration grouped by variant/skill).
- **Explicitly out:** migrations runner (→P2.2); capture writes (→P2.3+); any LLM at this tier ("No LLM at this tier" — §6).
- **Deltas touched:** **D2** (data model: `run_id` spine, `parent_event_id` event tree, replaces session/observation/summary/prompt); **D3** (sqlite-vec + ONNX, replaces Chroma).
- **pi-mem subsystem (KEEP/adapt):** KEEP SQLite stores + FTS5 (`src/services/sqlite/*`), Search-Strategy pattern (interface kept intact; **Chroma backend → sqlite-vec+ONNX is the "key swap seam"**, §B.1). KEEP context-injection formatters seam available.
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** embedding = 384-dim Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2 (§4.2); run reconstruction `any run_id → full ordered timeline + tree in < 200 ms` (§12.1); recall is indexed SQL + ANN, no LLM (§6).
- **Spike gating:** **gated on SPIKE-11** (sqlite-vec + ONNX MiniLM validation in Bun — QDS-EXEC-001 §7 L606-625; §D.5 DAG: "P2.1 ◀── SPIKE-11"). On red: Node-only fallback or swap to equivalent local 384-dim ONNX, log deviation.
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on P2.0; is the fan-out point for P2.3/P2.5/P2.6 and a dep for P2.4 and P2.12.

---

### P2.2 — Schema migration runner (3 pts · **vendor**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §4.3 (schema_meta / migrations) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.1 + §B.1

- **Design intent / scope:** Lift pi-mem's append-only `.sql` migrations runner; adapt to the new `schema_meta` table.
- **In scope:** append-only `.sql` files applied in order on startup; applied migrations recorded in `schema_meta`; seed rows `schema_version=1.0.0`, `engine=sqlite+sqlite-vec`, `embedding_model=Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`, `embedding_dim=384` (§4.3 verbatim INSERT).
- **Explicitly out:** schema content itself (owned by P2.1); any destructive/down-migrations (model is append-only).
- **Deltas touched:** supports D2 (versioned evolution of the new data model).
- **pi-mem subsystem (KEEP/adapt):** KEEP migrations dir + runner; adapt to new `schema_meta` (§B.1).
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** migrations are append-only and tracked in `schema_meta` (§4 + §4.3).
- **Spike gating:** none directly (rides P2.1's SPIKE-11 outcome).
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on P2.0; parallel sibling to P2.1 (§D.5: P2.0 fans to both P2.1 and P2.2).

---

### P2.3 — Tool-call capture (pi-mono ExtensionAPI) (3 pts · **glue**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §5.1 (signal 1) + §5.2 (threading) + §5.3 (adapters) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.1 (DoD verbatim) + §C D1/D4 + §A.3 (ExtensionAPI seam)

- **Design intent / scope:** Fresh-but-thin adapter on the **free pi-mono ExtensionAPI seam** (`tool_execution_*`) translating one tool invocation → one `events` row (+ one `event_vec` row), transactionally, **zero LLM**. This is the canonical capture pattern the other glue adapters follow.
- **In scope:** tool boundary → `events` (event_type `tool_call`) with full threading (run/agent/skill/prompt/parent — §5.2); ONNX embed at capture time; transactional write of event + vector.
- **Explicitly out:** any LLM distillation (that altitude moved to P2.7 nightly cron, D1); DAG/node concepts (→P2.4); IDE/transcript capture (stripped, §B.2).
- **Deltas touched:** **D1** (mechanical zero-LLM capture replacing per-event LLM); **D4** (capture surface = pi-mono ExtensionAPI, not IDE transcript-watching).
- **pi-mem subsystem (adapt):** retarget the pi-agent ExtensionAPI seam (`pi-agent/extensions/pi-mem.ts`) → capture entry point (§B.1).
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design (verbatim DoD, §D.1):** "**zero LLM/HTTP egress (network mock asserts); p99 < 5 ms over 1000 calls; ONNX deterministic 384-dim**." Reinforced by §12.1 ("Capture overhead per event < 5 ms p99, no per-event LLM"). Binds INV-OM1, INV-OM2, INV-OM3, INV-OM4.
- **Spike gating:** none (pi-mono seam is "free", §A.3) — but the ONNX/embed path rides the SPIKE-11 verdict from P2.1.
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on P2.1 (fan-out: "P2.3/P2.5/P2.6 fan out once P2.1 exists", §D.5).

---

### P2.4 — DAG-node capture (swarmclaw seam) (4 pts · **net-new**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §5.1 (signal 2) + §3.2 (handoffs) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.1 + §C D5 + QDS-EXEC-001 §7 SPIKE-10

- **Design intent / scope:** Net-new capture of swarmclaw DAG node transitions + inter-node handoffs → `node` events; reconstruct the event tree via `parent_event_id`. pi-mono has no DAG concept — these signals come from swarmclaw.
- **In scope:** node transitions + handoff payloads → `events` (event_type `node`); `parent_event_id` tree edges (node → tool call → subagent); optional `event_edges` `handoff` relations (§4.1).
- **Explicitly out:** building swarmclaw hook infrastructure (if absent, fall to pi-mono-level-only capture); A/B variant-tagged execution (→P2.10, though it shares the SPIKE-10 seam).
- **Deltas touched:** **D5** (DAG-node capture — no analog in pi-mono; from swarmclaw orchestration hook, shape unknown).
- **pi-mem subsystem:** none lifted (net-new); writes into the P2.1 store.
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** node transitions + handoffs → `node` events; event tree via `parent_event_id` (§D.1). Inherits the four invariants (esp. INV-OM1 zero-LLM, INV-OM2 run_id).
- **Spike gating:** **GATED on SPIKE-10** (swarmclaw DAG event-hook surface — QDS-EXEC-001 §7 L585-604; §D.5: "SPIKE-10 ──▶ P2.4"). **Fallback (SPIKE-10 red):** capture only pi-mono-level signals (P2.3/P2.5/P2.6) for the first Phase-2 pass; **defer P2.4** until the swarmclaw seam lands (QDS-EXEC-001 §7 SPIKE-10 fallback + R13).
- **Cross-epic deps:** also needs P2.1 ("P2.4 also needs P2.1", §D.5).

---

### P2.5 — Reasoning + agent-context capture (3 pts · **glue**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §5.1 (signal 3) + §2 (agent-reasoning overlap check) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.2 + §C D1/D4

- **Design intent / scope:** Glue adapter: pi-mono message events → `message` events; vector-indexed for semantic diagnosis. Captures the **raw reasoning stream** (Hindsight keeps only distilled durable decisions — the promotion gate distills raw→durable, §2).
- **In scope:** pi-mono message/decision/rationale events → `events` (event_type `message`) with full threading; ONNX embed → `event_vec` (embedded text per §4.2: `{event_type} {skill} {tool_name} {payload summary} {reasoning/message text}`).
- **Explicitly out:** distilling/summarizing reasoning (that's the nightly cron, D1); duplicating Hindsight's durable decision store (no overlap by design, §2).
- **Deltas touched:** **D1** (zero-LLM capture); **D4** (pi-mono ExtensionAPI `message_update` surface).
- **pi-mem subsystem (adapt):** same ExtensionAPI seam as P2.3.
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** message events vector-indexed (§D.2); same capture invariants as P2.3 (< 5 ms p99, zero-LLM, INV-OM1..4).
- **Spike gating:** none (pi-mono seam free); rides P2.1 SPIKE-11 for embed.
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on P2.1 (fan-out sibling of P2.3/P2.6).

---

### P2.6 — Outcome capture (3 pts · **glue**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §5.1 (signal 4) + §2 (outcomes overlap check) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.2 + §C D1

- **Design intent / scope:** Glue adapters for test/review/build signals → `outcome` events, linked to run. Operational keeps **raw per-run outcomes (internal)**; Hindsight keeps aggregated trends + external CodeRabbit (no overlap, §2).
- **In scope:** test-runner / review / build adapters → `events` (event_type `outcome`), `outcome ∈ {success, partial, failure, skipped, NULL}`, `error`, `result_json`; linked to run via `run_id` (and `runs.overall_outcome` rollup, §4.1).
- **Explicitly out:** trend aggregation / external review harvesting (Hindsight's Review Harvester owns external CodeRabbit, §2); LLM classification of outcomes (→ P2.7).
- **Deltas touched:** **D1** (mechanical capture).
- **pi-mem subsystem:** thin new adapters writing into the P2.1 store (glue, not lifted).
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** outcome events linked to run (§D.2); INV-OM1..4. Anti-success signal to avoid: adapters omitting threading fields make runs un-diagnosable (§12.3).
- **Spike gating:** none.
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on P2.1 (fan-out sibling).

---

### P2.7 — Nightly promotion cron (4 pts · **net-new**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §7.1 (cron) + §9 (cost model row) + §10 (promotion model/depth) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.2 + §C D1/D7

- **Design intent / scope:** Net-new cron that **replaces the stripped per-event LLM (D1)**. Once-nightly light consolidation of the day's events → one "Daily Learnings YYYY-MM-DD" document (structured JSON + prose). This is where ALL the tier's LLM intelligence lives.
- **In scope:** bounded-window read (scales with days, not events); light mechanical consolidation (dedupe, cluster by run/skill/topic, summarize each cluster); emit the dated doc; skip idle nights below event-volume threshold; cap doc size; tunable. (Full model in G.3 above.)
- **Explicitly out:** the `retain()` push to Hindsight (→P2.8); deep synthesis (stays in Hindsight weekly Synthesizer — promotion depth = **light** default, §10); any live/per-event handoff (batch only, §10 "Promotion model: overnight batch cron, no live handoff").
- **Deltas touched:** **D1** (all LLM moves here — "single most consequential change"); **D7** (resolves SPIKE-9; batch replaces live handoff).
- **pi-mem subsystem:** none lifted (net-new; conceptually replaces the stripped `SDKAgent` per-event observer, §B.2).
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** one LLM run/night, bounded doc size (§9 + §12.1); idle nights skipped; doc capped. Anti-success signal: "Nightly promotion docs grow unbounded → consolidation not deduping; tune cron" (§12.3).
- **Spike gating:** none directly (SPIKE-9 already RESOLVED — the cron model is the verdict, QDS-EXEC-001 §7 L552-583).
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on capture being live (P2.3/P2.5/P2.6) — §D.5: "P2.3/P2.5/P2.6 (capture live) ──▶ P2.7".

---

### P2.8 — Hindsight retain bridge (3 pts · **net-new**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §7.1 (step 4 routing) + §7.2 (bridge simplification) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.2 + §C D7 + QDS-MEM-001 §6.2 (routing table + I/O schema) + QDS-EXEC-001 §7 SPIKE-9

- **Design intent / scope:** Net-new bridge that pushes the Daily Learnings doc → Hindsight `retain()` into `qds-product`/`qds-meta` with correct tags. With this in place, QDS-MEM-001 §6.2's bridge no longer does live per-session LLM classification — it reads the pre-distilled doc (§7.2). **Resolves SPIKE-9 (D7).**
- **In scope:** route per §7.1 step 4 — product decisions/antipatterns/patterns → `qds-product` (tags `module:X`, `knowledge_type:Y`); skill/outcome/optimization → `qds-meta` (tags `skill:X`, `outcome:Y`, `quality_dim:Z`). Reuse the QDS-MEM-001 §6.2 routing/precedence table + I/O schema (still valid for this retain call — QDS-MEM-001 §6.2 cross-ref note L1580-1586).
- **Explicitly out:** changes to Hindsight banks/agents (purely additive — §13); the consolidation itself (→P2.7); the seeding script (one-time, QDS-MEM-001 §6.2 Sprint Artifact Ingestion — separate concern).
- **Deltas touched:** **D7** (Hindsight promotion — net-new, resolves SPIKE-9).
- **pi-mem subsystem:** none lifted (net-new).
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** retain volume ~1 doc/day, down from N raw events (§12.1); correct bank routing + tags (§7.1). Anti-success: experiments/promotion pipeline health metrics (§12.3).
- **Spike gating:** SPIKE-9 already RESOLVED (the batch cron IS the verdict).
- **Cross-epic deps:** **needs P1.11** (Hindsight prime/retain — KEEP; QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.2 "Cross-epic dep: P1.11"; §D.5 "P1.11 (KEEP, Hindsight retain) ──▶ P2.8"). Also depends on P2.7. (QDS-EXEC-001 §7 SPIKE-9 "Protects: P1.11".)

---

### P2.9 — Experiment propose + human gate (3 pts · **net-new**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §8.1 (steps 1-2) + §8.2 (the ONE human gate) + §4.1 `experiments` table + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.3 + §C D6 + §D.0 (merge of P3-OM-9 + P3-OM-10)

- **Design intent / scope:** Net-new, **merges propose + gate as one workflow unit** (§D.0). Optimizer agent scans recent runs → proposes hypothesis → `experiments` row (status `proposed`); then CLI/UI approve/reject + audit event → `approved | rejected`. This is **the single human gate** (§8.2).
- **In scope:** populate `experiments` (skill, hypothesis, variant_a_ref, variant_b_ref, metric ∈ {success_rate|token_cost|duration|review_findings}, status=proposed, proposed_at — §4.1); CLI/UI status flip → approved (set approved_at, approved_by) | rejected; **every transition emitted as an event** (§8.2 audit trail).
- **Explicitly out:** A/B execution + deploy (→P2.10); a second gate at deploy (explicitly rejected, §8.2 + §13); fully-autonomous proposal-to-deploy.
- **Deltas touched:** **D6** (experiment lifecycle — net-new; no pi-mem analog).
- **pi-mem subsystem:** none lifted (net-new).
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** one human gate at test-approval (§8.2); state transitions are audit events. Anti-success: "Experiments pile up in `proposed` with no human review → gate is a bottleneck or abandoned" (§12.3).
- **Spike gating:** none (proposal/gate are store-side; the *execution* it authorizes is what's spike-gated, in P2.10).
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on P2.8 (§D.5: "P2.8 ──▶ P2.9").

---

### P2.10 — A/B execution + deploy (5 pts · **net-new**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §8.1 (steps 3-4) + §4.1 `experiments` (deploy fields) + §8.2 + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.3 + §C D6 + §D.0 (merge of P3-OM-11 + P3-OM-12) + QDS-EXEC-001 §7 SPIKE-10

- **Design intent / scope:** Net-new, **merges execute + deploy** (§D.0). Swarmclaw runs N variant-tagged runs each, measures the metric, declares winner → `analyzed`; then deploys winner config + snapshots baseline + sets `confirm_until = +30d` → `deployed`.
- **In scope:** variant-tagged DAG runs (runs/events carry `variant` + `experiment_id`, §4.1); metric aggregation across variants → `winner ∈ {a,b,none}`, status `running → analyzed`; deploy: set `deploy_ref`, `baseline_metrics_json`, `deployed_at`, `confirm_until = deployed_at + 30d`, status `analyzed → deployed`.
- **Explicitly out:** the 30-day confirm/rollback (→P2.11); a human gate at deploy (rejected — winner deploys under the §8.2 approval).
- **Deltas touched:** **D6** (experiment lifecycle).
- **pi-mem subsystem:** none lifted (net-new).
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** approved experiment → analyzed winner within run-window (§12.1); baseline snapshotted at deploy. **Build strategy (§D.3): "ships interface + unit tests first; full E2E when seam confirmed."**
- **Spike gating:** **E2E GATED on SPIKE-10** (swarmclaw variant-tagged DAGs — §D.3 "E2E gated on swarmclaw variant-tagged DAGs (SPIKE-10)"; §D.5 "P2.10 (gated SPIKE-10)"). **Fallback (SPIKE-10 red):** ship interface + unit tests; defer the E2E A/B path until the seam lands (QDS-EXEC-001 §7 SPIKE-10 + R13).
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on P2.9 (§D.5: "P2.9 ──▶ P2.10").

---

### P2.11 — Confirm cron (30-day auto-rollback) (3 pts · **net-new**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §8.1 (step 5) + §8.2 (auto-rollback safety net) + §8.3 (loop closure) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.3 + §C D6

- **Design intent / scope:** Net-new cron: post-deploy metrics vs baseline; **confirm or auto-rollback**; emit a Hindsight `qds-meta` finding. The longitudinal safety net behind the single human gate (§8.2).
- **In scope:** read deployed experiments where `confirm_until` reached; compare post-deploy metrics vs `baseline_metrics_json`; status `deployed → confirming → confirmed | rolled_back`; on regression **revert `deploy_ref` automatically**; retain finding → `qds-meta` (`outcome:success` on confirm, `outcome:failure` on rollback — §8.1).
- **Explicitly out:** human approval of rollback (auto by design); the deploy itself (→P2.10).
- **Deltas touched:** **D6** (experiment lifecycle close).
- **pi-mem subsystem:** none lifted (net-new).
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** **≥ 95% of deployed experiments reach a confirmed/rolled_back verdict within 35 days** (§12.1); auto-rollback on regression (§8.2). Findings feed Meta-Optimizer's `intervention_effectiveness` (§8.3, cross-ref QDS-MEM-001 §4.3). Anti-success: "Deployed experiments never reach `confirmed`/`rolled_back` → 30-day cron not running or metrics missing" (§12.3).
- **Spike gating:** none (cron over the store).
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on P2.10 (§D.5: "P2.10 ──▶ P2.11"); writes to Hindsight `qds-meta` (relies on the P2.8 retain path / P1.11 being live).

---

### P2.12 — Replay + retention policy (3 pts · **net-new**)
**Spec Reference:** QDS-MEM-003 §6 (recall) + §12.1 (reconstruction target) + §13 (UI out of scope) + QDS-MEM-003-IMPL §D.4 + §B.3 (DEFER seam) + §D.0 (merge of P4-OM-14 + P4-OM-15)

- **Design intent / scope:** Net-new housekeeping, **merges replay/debug + retention/observability** (§D.0 — both are operations over the same event store). Reconstruct any run's event timeline/tree for diagnosis (serves optimizer + humans); operational-store cleanup/decay policy.
- **In scope:** replay API — reconstruct any run's ordered event timeline + `parent_event_id` tree; retention/decay cleanup policy (the tier is "ephemeral-leaning, decays" — §2 lifetime row); basic metrics/observability.
- **Explicitly out:** **graphical viewer UI** (explicitly out of scope — §13 "UI for replay/debug … a graphical viewer is a future enhancement"; the DEFER worker HTTP/SSE daemon + viewer UI plugs in **behind** this replay API later — §B.3: "replay API (P2.12) exposes the data; UI is a later layer").
- **Deltas touched:** none new (operates over D2 data); enables the deferred Phase-4 tooling seam (§B.3).
- **pi-mem subsystem (DEFER seam):** the worker HTTP/SSE daemon + viewer UI (`src/services/worker/http/*`, `src/ui/viewer/*`) are **DEFERred** and plug in behind this story's replay API (§B.3).
- **Acceptance constraints stated in design:** "any run_id → full ordered event timeline + tree in < 200 ms" (§12.1); any DAG run replayable/diagnosable at tool-call granularity (§12.2).
- **Spike gating:** none.
- **Cross-epic deps:** depends on P2.1 (§D.5: "P2.1 ──▶ P2.12").

# QDS-MEM-003 Phase-2 — Data Model & Vendored-Source Map (Research Agent 3 deliverable)

## CRITICAL ORIENTATION FOR WRITING AGENTS

The vendored substrate is **NOT at a path named `pi-mem`**. The design doc's "pi-mem" paths (`src/services/sqlite/*`, `src/servers/mcp-server.ts`, etc.) map onto the repo at:

- **`/Users/steve/QDS-Factory-Old/claude-mem/`** — the actual vendored substrate (claude-mem v10.5.2; pi-mem IS claude-mem retargeted). Source layout is **`claude-mem/src/services/...`**, not `src/services/...`. There is no directory literally named `pi-mem` anywhere in QDS-Factory-Old (confirmed via `find -iname "*pi-mem*"` → empty).
- **`/Users/steve/QDS-Factory-Old/pi-mono/`** — the pi-agent monorepo. The "free ExtensionAPI seam" (D4) lives at `pi-mono/packages/coding-agent/src/core/extensions/types.ts`. There is **no `pi-agent/extensions/pi-mem.ts`** file (the design doc's assumed path does not exist — flag for writers).
- Runtime: **Bun** (`import { Database } from 'bun:sqlite'`), TypeScript, ESM (`.js` import suffixes).

---

## PART 1 — TARGET OPERATIONAL-STORE SCHEMA (from QDS-MEM-003 §4)

The design specifies the schema **fully and concretely** — writers should treat §4 (design doc lines 114-233) as the literal DDL target. Reproduced with invariant annotations:

### Table `events` (the captured-signal spine)
```sql
CREATE TABLE events (
  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,          -- {domain}:{uuid}, domain∈(tool,node,msg,outcome)
  run_id          TEXT NOT NULL,             -- FK runs  [INV-OM2: non-null on EVERY row]
  dag_node_id     TEXT,                      -- which DAG node produced this (D5/SPIKE-10)
  parent_event_id TEXT,                      -- [D2] tree edge (node → tool call → subagent)
  agent_id        TEXT NOT NULL,
  agent_role      TEXT,                      -- coder | reviewer | planner
  skill           TEXT,
  skill_version   TEXT,
  prompt_id       TEXT,
  prompt_version  TEXT,
  variant         TEXT,                      -- A/B tag (NULL if not in experiment)
  experiment_id   TEXT,                      -- FK experiments (NULL if not in experiment)
  event_type      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(event_type IN ('tool_call','node','message','outcome')),
  tool_name       TEXT,
  payload_json    TEXT NOT NULL,             -- [INV-OM3: ≤64KB, secret-masked before write]
  result_json     TEXT,                      -- [INV-OM3: ≤64KB, secret-masked before write]
  tokens          INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  duration_ms     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  outcome         TEXT,                      -- success|partial|failure|skipped|NULL
  error           TEXT,
  created_at      INTEGER NOT NULL           -- epoch ms
);
-- 9 indexes: run, agent, skill, type, variant, experiment, parent, created (design L141-148)
```
Invariant annotations: **INV-OM2** (`run_id` non-null — the spine); **INV-OM3** (`payload_json`/`result_json` ≤64KB + secret-pattern-masked); **D2** (`parent_event_id` builds the event tree); threading contract §5.2 (every row carries run_id/agent_id/created_at + applicable skill/prompt/variant/experiment).

### Table `runs` (one row per executed DAG)
```sql
CREATE TABLE runs (
  run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, dag_def_id TEXT NOT NULL, variant TEXT, experiment_id TEXT,
  started_at INTEGER NOT NULL, ended_at INTEGER,
  status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('running','completed','failed','aborted')),
  total_tokens INTEGER DEFAULT 0, files_touched_json TEXT, overall_outcome TEXT
);  -- 3 indexes: dag_def_id, variant, started_at
```

### Table `experiments` (A/B state machine — design §8)
```sql
CREATE TABLE experiments (
  experiment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, skill TEXT NOT NULL, hypothesis TEXT NOT NULL,
  variant_a_ref TEXT NOT NULL, variant_b_ref TEXT NOT NULL, metric TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'proposed'
    CHECK(status IN ('proposed','approved','running','analyzed','deployed',
                     'confirming','confirmed','rolled_back','rejected')),
  winner TEXT, proposed_fix TEXT, deploy_ref TEXT, baseline_metrics_json TEXT,
  proposed_at INTEGER NOT NULL, approved_at INTEGER, approved_by TEXT,
  executed_at INTEGER, deployed_at INTEGER, confirm_until INTEGER,  -- =deployed_at+30d
  decided_at INTEGER, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);  -- 2 indexes: status, skill
```
9-state machine (design L177-179): proposed→approved|rejected→running→analyzed→deployed→confirming→confirmed|rolled_back.

### Table `event_edges` (cross-links beyond the parent tree)
```sql
CREATE TABLE event_edges (
  source_id TEXT NOT NULL, target_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  relation TEXT NOT NULL,   -- handoff | caused_by | spawned | produced
  PRIMARY KEY (source_id, target_id, relation)
);  -- 2 indexes: source, target
```

### Virtual table `event_vec` (sqlite-vec — D3/INV-OM4)
```sql
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE event_vec USING vec0(
  event_id  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  embedding FLOAT[384]      -- Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2, local ONNX
);
```
**D3/INV-OM4**: 384-dim, embeddings generated at capture from a **pinned local ONNX path — no remote fetch**. Embedded text = `{event_type} {skill} {tool_name} {payload summary} {reasoning/message text}` (design L219).

### Table `schema_meta` (migration tracking)
```sql
CREATE TABLE schema_meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL);
-- seeds: schema_version=1.0.0, engine=sqlite+sqlite-vec,
--        embedding_model=Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2, embedding_dim=384
```
Migrations are append-only `.sql` applied in order on startup; applied set recorded in `schema_meta`.

### GAPS writers MUST fill (design under-specifies)
1. **FTS5 is named but never DDL'd.** Design §4 mentions FTS5 only in the §4 header prose ("plus … FTS5"); §4.1-4.3 give NO `CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE … USING fts5`. The vendored claude-mem FTS5 pattern (content-table + sync triggers, below) is the template — **writers must author the events FTS5 table + triggers themselves.** (P2.1)
2. **No FK declarations** in the DDL (run_id/experiment_id are "FK" in comments only). Vendored substrate uses real `FOREIGN KEY … ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE` — writers decide whether to enforce.
3. **`schema_meta` shape mismatch** with vendored runner: claude-mem uses an integer-versioned `schema_versions(id,version,applied_at)` table + idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, NOT a key/value `schema_meta`. P2.2 must reconcile (adapt runner to the design's `schema_meta`, or adopt both).
4. **64KB enforcement + secret-masking mechanism** (INV-OM3) is unspecified — no helper in vendored source; net-new in P2.3.
5. **`dag_node_id` source shape unknown** — SPIKE-10 gates P2.4 (pi-mono has no DAG concept; confirmed `grep DAG` → empty).

---

## PART 2 — VENDORED-SOURCE MAP (real paths + code shapes, KEEP/STRIP)

### KEEP — lift + adapt

**[KEEP] SQLite stores + FTS5** — `/Users/steve/QDS-Factory-Old/claude-mem/src/services/sqlite/`
Key files: `SessionStore.ts` (main store, ~900 lines, holds schema + inline migrations + FTS5), `Database.ts` (defines `Migration` type), `SessionSearch.ts` (FTS5 MATCH queries), `transactions.ts`, `types.ts`, and subdirs `observations/ prompts/ sessions/ summaries/ timeline/`.
`SessionStore.ts` shape:
```ts
export class SessionStore {
  public db: Database;
  constructor(dbPath: string = DB_PATH) {
    this.db = new Database(dbPath);
    this.db.run('PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL');     // ← WAL per design §4
    this.db.run('PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL');
    this.db.run('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON');
    this.initializeSchema();   // CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS (idempotent)
    this.ensureWorkerPortColumn(); /* …14 inline ALTER-style migrations… */
  }
}
```
FTS5 template (the pattern to copy for `events`, since design omits it) — `SessionStore.ts:434-460`:
```sql
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE user_prompts_fts USING fts5(
  prompt_text, content='user_prompts', content_rowid='id');
-- + AFTER INSERT/DELETE/UPDATE triggers syncing fts ('delete' sentinel on del/upd)
```
Note: claude-mem wraps FTS5 creation in try/catch ("FTS5 not available … search uses ChromaDB" — `SessionStore.ts:461`). In QDS, sqlite-vec replaces Chroma, so FTS5 should be required, not optional.

**[KEEP] Migrations runner (append-only + runner)** — `/Users/steve/QDS-Factory-Old/claude-mem/src/services/sqlite/migrations.ts` + `migrations/runner.ts`
`migrations.ts` shape: numbered `Migration` objects `export const migration001: Migration = { version: 1, up: (db: Database) => { db.run(\`CREATE TABLE…\`) } }`. `migrations/runner.ts`:
```ts
export class MigrationRunner {
  constructor(private db: Database) {}
  runAllMigrations(): void {
    this.initializeSchema();            // creates schema_versions(id,version,applied_at)
    this.ensureWorkerPortColumn(); …    // ordered, idempotent steps
  }
}
```
**ADAPT (P2.2):** swap the integer `schema_versions` table for the design's key/value `schema_meta`; replace claude-mem's domain tables with events/runs/experiments/event_edges/event_vec; convert inline `ensureXColumn()` methods into discrete append-only `.sql` files (design mandates append-only `.sql`).

**[KEEP] Async queue processor w/ backpressure** — `/Users/steve/QDS-Factory-Old/claude-mem/src/services/queue/SessionQueueProcessor.ts`
This is the design's "SessionQueueProcessor.ts" — exact name, single file. Shape:
```ts
export class SessionQueueProcessor {
  constructor(private store: PendingMessageStore, private events: EventEmitter) {}
  async *createIterator(opts: CreateIteratorOptions): AsyncIterableIterator<PendingMessageWithId> {
    while (!signal.aborted) {
      const msg = this.store.claimNextMessage(sessionDbId); // atomic claim-confirm, self-heals stale
      if (msg) yield this.toPendingMessageWithId(msg);
      else await this.waitForMessage(signal, IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS); // event-driven backpressure
    }
  }
}
```
Backpressure = DB-backed atomic claim/confirm + EventEmitter wake-up + 3-min idle timeout. Backing store is `src/services/sqlite/PendingMessageStore.ts` (a `pending_messages` table, `SessionStore.ts:525`).
